# The Beyond: picture list for an illustrated edition

642 images. Every row in `asset-list.csv` is one picture, with its file name, size and a description to turn into a prompt.

## Version 5

Every row is marked to make again except the patrons and the title screen, which keep the rulebook's own art.

**New columns in the CSV:** `era` (medieval, victorian, wars, atomic, modern, now, or items), `kind` (portrait, monster, place, item, emblem, hideout), `year` (the case's year, when it has one), `variant` (`late` marks Late Century 2007-2021: smartphones, flat screens, LED bulbs), `hero` (the one subject of the picture), `mood` (`bright` marks daylight pictures, so the dark lead can be left off), and `seed` (blank: fill it to re-roll a picture without rewording it).

**What changed from v4**
- Rows no longer repeat the era's light and palette: the generator adds those. Rows keep concrete period things (furniture, vehicles, appliances) instead.
- Generic rooms and every indoor theme (cellar, church, lab, train, village) list indoor things for their era. They no longer borrow the street list.
- Late Century is split: 1990s and early 2000s, and 2007-2021 (the Vacancy case, 2016). Generic Late Century rows are the 1990s look.
- No readable text in any prompt (signs, labels, headlines, countdowns): shapes only. At most three weighted phrases per prompt.
- Portraits vary in head angle, expression, build and one worn detail, so people of one era no longer look cloned.
- A character who appears in several cases of a campaign has one row and one picture (marked Recurring). The game shows that picture in every case.
- The drive-in screen row is rewritten (blazing white, black screen in the negative). Fight backdrops no longer ask for a plain bottom third: the game darkens them.

### Characters: Death of a Bloodline (1976)

- `portraits/npc/death-of-a-bloodline-haruka.png` (1024x1024): **Haruka Mori, Investigator, 34**. head-and-shoulders portrait of Haruka Mori, Investigator, 34. Eleven years in Tokyo Metropolitan homicide before a case with no body and too many witnesses brought the EHG to her door. Still carries her badge out of habit. (Clothing of the 1950s to 1980s:1.2), in profile, looking away, undefined, a dim background with a blurred neon sign and a television glow. Keep out: (signature, text, watermark:1.5), frame, cartoon, 3d render, photograph.
- `portraits/npc/death-of-a-bloodline-kenji.png` (1024x1024): **Kenji Arai, Priest, 41**. head-and-shoulders portrait of Kenji Arai, Priest, 41. Third-generation priest of a small Inari shrine in Ochanomizu. Joined the Guard in 1959 after the shrine's foxes stopped staying still at night. (Clothing of the 1950s to 1980s:1.2), looking over one shoulder, haunted, hollow-eyed, a dim background with a blurred neon sign and a television glow. Keep out: (signature, text, watermark:1.5), frame, cartoon, 3d render, photograph.
- `portraits/npc/death-of-a-bloodline-daichi.png` (1024x1024): **Daichi "Ox" Kuroda, Guardian, 28**. head-and-shoulders portrait of Daichi "Ox" Kuroda, Guardian, 28. Riot police, Shinjuku, until the night a crowd of commuters turned to face the wrong way all at once. He held the stairwell. Nobody else on his squad remembers it. (Clothing of the 1950s to 1980s:1.2), three-quarters left, eyes down, undefined, a dim background with a blurred neon sign and a television glow. Keep out: (signature, text, watermark:1.5), frame, cartoon, 3d render, photograph.
- `portraits/npc/death-of-a-bloodline-nomura.png` (1024x1024): **Daisuke Nomura, Homicide, Tokyo Metropolitan Police · 49**. head-and-shoulders portrait of Daisuke Nomura, Homicide, Tokyo Metropolitan Police · 49. Thick-built and clean-shaven, salt-and-pepper hair combed back. A canvas trench coat over rumpled shirt sleeves; his wire-rimmed glasses fog in the rain. Cigarettes in a flat metal case beside his notebook. (Clothing of the 1950s to 1980s:1.2), head turned three-quarters to the right, undefined, a dim background with a blurred neon sign and a television glow. Keep out: (signature, text, watermark:1.5), frame, cartoon, 3d render, photograph.
- `portraits/npc/death-of-a-bloodline-kaede.png` (1024x1024): **Kaede Yamada, 2B · in custody · 36**. head-and-shoulders portrait of Kaede Yamada, 2B · in custody · 36. Tired and pale, black hair loosely tied, a municipal-issued robe. Her hands are folded tightly in her lap and her eyes keep to the table. She tenses at sharp sounds. (Clothing of the 1950s to 1980s:1.2), facing the viewer, chin slightly raised, undefined, a dim background with a blurred neon sign and a television glow. Keep out: (signature, text, watermark:1.5), frame, cartoon, 3d render, photograph.
- `portraits/npc/death-of-a-bloodline-naoko.png` (1024x1024): **Naoko Fukuda, 1A · 33**. head-and-shoulders portrait of Naoko Fukuda, 1A · 33. A young widow with tired eyes and shoulder-length hair tied back, a plain cardigan. She speaks gently but quickly, glancing back toward her silent toddler. (Clothing of the 1950s to 1980s:1.2), head turned three-quarters to the right, suspicious, one brow raised, a dim background with a blurred neon sign and a television glow. Keep out: (signature, text, watermark:1.5), frame, cartoon, 3d render, photograph.
- `portraits/npc/death-of-a-bloodline-hideki.png` (1024x1024): **Hideki Masuda, 1B · 41**. head-and-shoulders portrait of Hideki Masuda, 1B · 41. Gaunt and pale, shaved head, narrow eyes. Layered jackets and threadbare slippers. He keeps to the corner of the doorway and doesn't speak unless spoken to. (Clothing of the 1950s to 1980s:1.2), head turned three-quarters to the left, undefined, a dim background with a blurred neon sign and a television glow. Keep out: (signature, text, watermark:1.5), frame, cartoon, 3d render, photograph.
- `portraits/npc/death-of-a-bloodline-kenjiro.png` (1024x1024): **Kenjiro Watanabe, 1D · 67**. head-and-shoulders portrait of Kenjiro Watanabe, 1D · 67. (Clothing of the 1950s to 1980s:1.2), in profile, looking away, undefined, a dim background with a blurred neon sign and a television glow. Keep out: (signature, text, watermark:1.5), frame, cartoon, 3d render, photograph.
- `portraits/npc/death-of-a-bloodline-daigo.png` (1024x1024): **Daigo Shimizu, 2A · 29**. head-and-shoulders portrait of Daigo Shimizu, 2A · 29. (Clothing of the 1950s to 1980s:1.2), facing the viewer, chin slightly raised, undefined, a dim background with a blurred neon sign and a television glow. Keep out: (signature, text, watermark:1.5), frame, cartoon, 3d render, photograph.
- `portraits/npc/death-of-a-bloodline-chihiro.png` (1024x1024): **Chihiro Ishikawa, 2C · 24**. head-and-shoulders portrait of Chihiro Ishikawa, 2C · 24. ### Characters: The Bell of Saint-Rémy (1187)

### Places: Death of a Bloodline (1976)

- `scenes/death-of-a-bloodline-street.png` (1344x768): **street**. a rain-soaked Tokyo backstreet at night outside an old two-storey apartment block, 1976. Wide establishing view, atmospheric.
- `scenes/death-of-a-bloodline-station.png` (1344x768): **station**. a police interview room, Kanda Station, 1976, fluorescent light. Wide establishing view, atmospheric.
- `scenes/death-of-a-bloodline-hall1.png` (1344x768): **hall1**. ground floor hallway of a shabby 1970s Tokyo apartment building, doors along one side. Wide establishing view, atmospheric.
- `scenes/death-of-a-bloodline-hall2.png` (1344x768): **hall2**. second floor hallway, same building, a door at the end left ajar. Wide establishing view, atmospheric.
- `scenes/death-of-a-bloodline-hall3.png` (1344x768): **hall3**. third floor hallway, same building, dim and silent. Wide establishing view, atmospheric.
- `scenes/death-of-a-bloodline-doorway.png` (1344x768): **doorway**. an apartment doorway seen from the hall, a neighbour's door half open. Wide establishing view, atmospheric.
- `scenes/death-of-a-bloodline-apt2b_living.png` (1344x768): **apt2b_living**. a small Tokyo apartment living room, tatami, a low table, everything too neat. Wide establishing view, atmospheric.
- `scenes/death-of-a-bloodline-apt2b_kitchen.png` (1344x768): **apt2b_kitchen**. a narrow apartment kitchen, dishes in the sink, a calendar on the wall. Wide establishing view, atmospheric.
- `scenes/death-of-a-bloodline-apt2b_bedroom.png` (1344x768): **apt2b_bedroom**. a small bedroom with futon rolled away, a child's drawings on the wall. Wide establishing view, atmospheric.
- `scenes/death-of-a-bloodline-apt2b_storage.png` (1344x768): **apt2b_storage**. a cramped storage cupboard full of boxes and folded paper. Wide establishing view, atmospheric.
- `scenes/death-of-a-bloodline-apt2a.png` (1344x768): **apt2a**. a young courier's messy apartment, posters, a motorbike helmet. Wide establishing view, atmospheric.
- `scenes/death-of-a-bloodline-basement.png` (1344x768): **basement**. a dark boiler-room basement under the building, pipes and a locked door. Wide establishing view, atmospheric.
- `scenes/death-of-a-bloodline-paper_guest.png` (1344x768): **paper_guest**. a guest room made entirely of folded white paper, uncanny. Wide establishing view, atmospheric.
- `scenes/death-of-a-bloodline-paper_dojo.png` (1344x768): **paper_dojo**. a dojo made entirely of folded paper, white lilies drifting. Wide establishing view, atmospheric.
- `scenes/death-of-a-bloodline-paper_study.png` (1344x768): **paper_study**. a study made of folded paper, shelves of paper books. Wide establishing view, atmospheric.
